# Agents · G · part 2

947 entries, game-ux-designer to gateway-route-guard. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **game-ux-designer**: GameForge UX Designer (Mira Osei) with project memory, for an independent review or deliverable on onboarding, HUD and menu flows, usability, and player feedback. Used by /gameforge:game-team-orchestrator for parallel consultations. ([AlterLab-IEU/AlterLab_GameForge](https://github.com/AlterLab-IEU/AlterLab_GameForge/blob/HEAD/agents/game-ux-designer.md) · ★56)
- **game-ux-designer**: UX/UI and game-identity reviewer for game mode and the main app: information hierarchy, first-run onboarding, noise, readability at a glance, accessibility, and per-page Game Identity score (is it visibly a game?). Runs on every frontend change. Read-only. ([frmingest/Aladdin](https://github.com/frmingest/Aladdin/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/game-ux-designer.md))
- **game-verifier**: Runs the OSRS tower-defense verification gate (tsc, vitest, static export) and, when the change touches the UI or the engine, drives the exported game in a headless browser to observe it. Returns a terse verdict plus any failures — build logs, DOM dumps and browser stack traces stay in its own context. Use before committing a non-trivial change, or whenever a claim that something "works" needs evidence. ([hamilton-junior/osrs-tower-defense](https://github.com/hamilton-junior/osrs-tower-defense/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/game-verifier.md) · ★3)
- **game-verifier**: Verifier of the di-games routine. Use after the builder hands over a stage (greybox, playable v1, look), or to run playtest sessions. Starts with a fresh context, checks the stage's gate itself, and signs off or sends the work back. Never builds. ([dob-0/di.iiii](https://github.com/dob-0/di.iiii/blob/HEAD/plugins/di-games/agents/game-verifier.md) · ★1)
- **game-visual-designer**: Expert consultant for game visual design, graphics architecture, and visual effects planning. Use proactively for visual design analysis, graphics optimization recommendations, sprite and animation planning, art direction guidance, and visual accessibility consulting. When you prompt this agent, describe exactly what you want them to do in as much detail as necessary. Remember, this agent has no context about any questions or previous conversations between you and the user. So be sure to communicate clearly, and provide all relevant context. ([spacehendrix/clauder](https://github.com/spacehendrix/clauder/blob/HEAD/.claude-expansion-packs/game-dev/agents/game-visual-designer.md) · ★59)
- **game-visual-identity-consultant**: Establish art direction and visual cohesion. Use this agent when defining visual style, designing memorable characters, creating color systems, or ensuring visual consistency across the game. - **gate-runner**: Runs the full deterministic gate and returns only the failures. Use to verify a slice without polluting the main context with build output. ([seulnan/tomolog-server](https://github.com/seulnan/tomolog-server/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/gate-runner.md))
- **gate-runner**: Runs the gates a brief names (backend and frontend tests, lint, E2E, the API-client staleness check, the hook cases) and reports their real output. Fixes nothing. ([tarka1939/My_Site](https://github.com/tarka1939/My_Site/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/gate-runner.md))
- **gate-runner**: Runs one trevokeyless gate set (rust, python, ts, scripts, or cross) — the local CI-equivalent commands — and returns a compact pass/fail report. Use to validate changes without flooding the caller with build and test logs. ([trevofinance/trevokeyless](https://github.com/trevofinance/trevokeyless/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/gate-runner.md))
- **gate-runner**: Mechanical first tier of agent supervision. Runs .supervision/bin/gate.sh on one builder commit and returns a compact PASS/FAIL table of tests, lint, every probe, removed/skipped tests, secret scan and protected paths. Never judges the diff, never writes a verdict, never edits files. Invoke from the agent-supervisor skill on every READY_FOR_REVIEW or new commit. ([umairmsm/agent-loop-kit](https://github.com/umairmsm/agent-loop-kit/blob/HEAD/agents/gate-runner.md))
- **gate-runner**: Runs the suite's browser gates (accessibility at runtime, Core Web Vitals, the state-matrix diff) on a served or built page and returns only the verdicts, the failing findings and the JSON paths, so thousands of lines of output stay out of the main conversation. ([Vybecode-LTD/Pro-Web-Designer-Suite-Plug-in](https://github.com/Vybecode-LTD/Pro-Web-Designer-Suite-Plug-in/blob/HEAD/plugins/pro-web-design-suite/agents/gate-runner.md) · 2 copies)
- **gate-runner**: Run this repository's full CI gate — both the Python/docs half and the frontend half — and report a per-command exit-code table with only the failing excerpt. Delegate every gate run here: the raw output is hundreds of lines that would otherwise sit in the main thread's context for the rest of the session. Returns evidence, never a verdict on whether the work is done. ([yes-404/gi-pricing-plan](https://github.com/yes-404/gi-pricing-plan/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/gate-runner.md))
- **gate-scout**: Discover the repository's build, lint, unit test, integration, coverage, CI ownership, sensor-scope, and runtime-health commands without executing them. ([macatocz/makato-oss](https://github.com/macatocz/makato-oss/blob/HEAD/plugins/makato-build/agents/gate-scout.md))
- **gate-sdd-reviewer**: Strict, read-only reviewer for the gate-oriented-sdd harness itself. - **gate-skeptic**: Adversarial reviewer for a ProofGate delivery. Use before declaring a delivery done, in parallel with verify.sh. ([ChrnX0/proofgate](https://github.com/ChrnX0/proofgate/blob/HEAD/agents/gate-skeptic.md) · ★1)
